### [1] [SynWeaver: Website-Prior Task and Trajectory Co-Synthesis for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12429)
*Ruitao Wang,Yuwen Hao,Menglin Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SynWeaver是一个网站先验知识-任务轨迹协同合成框架，通过结构化网站探索构建网站地图，训练UI感知模型以生成更真实的网站任务，并通过协同合成与验证修复机制生成高质量、可执行的监督数据，在WebArena和WebVoyager上显著优于现有合成基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网页代理（Web agents）在未见过的网站上泛化能力不足，因为缺乏网站特定的监督信息。现有的基于探索的数据合成方法虽然减少了人工标注，但仍存在两个关键局限：一是难以覆盖网站的全部功能；二是缺乏网站先验知识，容易提出幻觉任务（hallucinated tasks），从而限制了轨迹合成的多样性和效率。

Method: SynWeaver采用三阶段框架：(1) 结构化网站探索：构建一个网站地图，覆盖网站上功能各异的页面状态和可执行交互；(2) 基于网站先验的UI感知模型训练：从网站地图中提取页面级和转换级监督信息，训练一个具有网站特定先验的UI感知模型，用于生成更真实的（grounded）任务提议；(3) 协同任务-轨迹合成：联合更新任务与其执行轨迹以解决二者不一致的问题，并通过验证和修复机制生成可执行、语义对齐的监督数据。

Result: 在WebArena和WebVoyager两个基准上的实验表明，SynWeaver一致优于强合成基线方法，并且为域内和域外泛化均能产生更有效的监督数据。具体表现为任务建议更真实、轨迹可执行性更高、监督质量更好。

Conclusion: SynWeaver通过将网站探索、先验建模与轨迹合成有机结合，有效解决了现有数据合成方法中功能覆盖不全和任务幻觉的问题，显著提升了网页代理在未见网站上的泛化能力，为网页代理的自动化监督数据合成提供了新思路。

Abstract: Web agents often struggle to generalize to unseen websites because they lack website-specific supervision. Recent exploration-based data synthesis methods reduce manual annotation, but they still face two key limitations: they often fail to cover the full functionality of a website, and without sufficient website prior knowledge, they tend to propose hallucinated tasks, which in turn limits the diversity and efficiency of downstream trajectory synthesis. We present \textbf{SynWeaver}, a website-prior task-trajectory co-synthesis framework designed to address these challenges. SynWeaver first performs structured website exploration and constructs a website map that covers a broad set of functionally distinct page states and executable interactions on the target website. It then derives page-level and transition-level supervision from this map to train a UI-aware model with website-specific priors, enabling more grounded task proposals. Finally, SynWeaver performs collaborative task-trajectory synthesis, jointly updating the task and execution trajectory when they become inconsistent, and then verifies and repairs the collected results to produce executable, semantically aligned supervision. Experiments on WebArena and WebVoyager demonstrate that SynWeaver consistently outperforms strong synthesis baselines and yields more effective supervision for both in-domain and out-of-domain generalization.

</details>


### [2] [Specification-first convergence with an AI coding agent: a case study of dismantling a core architectural invariant across 189 files in a 717k-line codebase with no test oracle and no human code review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12440)
*Joel Abenhaim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文报告了一个完全仪表化的案例研究：AI编码代理在规范优先协议下，对71.7万行TypeScript生产应用执行大规模架构重构，无人审查生成代码、也无预存oracle验证目标行为。任务（拆除贯穿大型相互依赖代码库的核心生命周期不变量）被作者评估为通过增量重构实际不可行、传统上需要重写，但该代理在协议下成功完成。整个变更涉及189个文件（31个新增），两次提交共288个文件、34,770行插入、16,422行删除；三天完成，成本2,430美元，期间无观测到bug。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索AI编码代理在无人工代码审查、无预存测试oracle的条件下，能否自主完成传统上被认为必须重写的大规模架构重构。作者刻意选择了一个通过增量重构被认为不可行的任务，以检验AI代理是否能在严肃的生产级代码库上超越传统重构的可行性边界。

Method: 采用规范优先（specification-first）协议：(1) 代理先进行正式规范编写；(2) 14轮精化循环审计规范与源代码的一致性；(3) 原子化实现；(4) 编译/测试反馈循环；(5) 17轮验证循环审计代码与冻结规范的一致性。共31次审计遍历，修正了201个缺陷后才由人类首次执行程序。收敛标准为经验性的：连续两轮验证返回零发现。

Result: 变更触及189个文件（31个新增），抽取阶段合并后两次提交共288个文件、34,770行插入、16,422行删除。在首次会话及后续约30次会话中，软件行为符合规范，未观测到任何bug。总耗时三天，成本2,430美元。

Conclusion: 该案例表明，在严格的规范优先协议下，AI编码代理可以在无人审查代码、无预存oracle的情况下，成功完成传统上被认为不可行的大规模架构重构。以形式化规范审计为中枢的质量保障循环（而非依赖测试或人工评审）足以支撑大型生产级重构任务的可靠性。

Abstract: This paper reports a single, fully instrumented case study of a large-scale architectural refactoring by an AI coding agent under a specification-first protocol, with no human review of the generated code and no pre-existing oracle to validate the target behaviour. The task, dismantling a central invariant across a large interdependent codebase, was assessed by the author as effectively infeasible through incremental refactoring, the kind of change that conventionally calls for a rewrite instead. Under the protocol described here, the agent completed it successfully.
  The system is a 717,725-line production TypeScript application across 3,648 files. The task required dismantling a core lifetime invariant: the guarantee that a UI panel remains open for the duration of an AI request. The target behaviour was that a streaming generation survives the closing of its panel and can be reattached, on reopening, to the same live stream with no loss or duplication.
  The protocol: formal specification by the agent, 14 refinement cycles auditing that specification against the source code, atomic implementation, a compile/test feedback loop, then 17 verification cycles auditing the code against the frozen specification. Across 31 audit passes, 201 defects were corrected before any human executed the program. The convergence criterion was empirical: two consecutive verification passes returning zero findings.
  The change touched 189 files (31 new); with the extraction phase, the two commits total 288 files, 34,770 insertions, 16,422 deletions. Across the first and roughly thirty later sessions, the software behaved as specified, no bug observed. Elapsed: three days; cost: USD 2,430.
  The full specification and raw session logs, 1,500+ pages in French, are published as evidence, allowing inspection of the process and submission to a language model for consistency checking.

</details>


### [3] [Does It Render Everywhere? A Study of Cross-Environment Compatibility in MLLM-Generated Webpages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12518)
*Ziyun Guo,Jingyu Xiao,Yuqiang Sun,Yintong Huo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地研究AI生成网页的跨环境兼容性问题。作者构建了WebCompat数据集（含2,032个标注实例，覆盖8种AI工具生成的网页在9种浏览器/设备组合下的渲染），发现68%的生成网页存在至少一个兼容性问题，其中88.3%的问题是破坏整页布局的严重故障。基于发现，作者开发了XCompat轻量级检测器，结合视觉截图和DOM树分析，在WebCompat-test上达到0.903的F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM生成网页的评估仅关注固定浏览器设备配置下的视觉保真度，忽视了真实部署场景中跨环境渲染兼容性的关键问题。这一评估缺口使得AI生成前端代码在实际应用中的可靠性缺乏保障。

Method: 构建WebCompat数据集：收集8个代表性AI工具生成的网页，在9种浏览器和设备组合下渲染，进行2,032个实例的标注分析。从兼容性问题普遍性、用户可感知症状和代码级根因三个层面进行分析。基于发现提出XCompat检测器，融合视觉截图与DOM结构树进行轻量级兼容性问题检测。

Result: 68%的生成网页存在至少一个兼容性问题；最普遍的故障症状是破坏整页布局（88.3%），包括页面直接缩小适配导致字体过小或缩放比例失衡导致内容截断；元素级故障（如图像变形、组件缺失）相对较少（13.4%）。XCompat在WebCompat-test上F1达0.903，优于现有兼容性检查工具和LLM基线。

Conclusion: MLLM生成前端代码存在系统性跨环境兼容性问题，尽管多数模型已融入响应式设计模式生成，但代码实现不够正确。XCompat作为高效的兼容性检测工具可辅助提升AI生成网页的渲染可靠性，数据集和工具已开源以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have been increasingly adopted to automate webpage generation from visual designs (e.g., screenshots). However, existing evaluations are limited to visual fidelity assessment under a fixed browser-device configuration. Such a setting overlooks the cross-environment rendering compatibility for real-world deployments.
  To address this gap, we present the first systematic empirical study of cross-environment compatibility in AI-generated webpages. Specifically, we construct WebCompat, a dataset of 2,032 annotated instances, comprising webpages generated by 8 representative AI tools, each rendered across 9 browser-and-device combinations. We analyze the prevalence of compatibility issues, their user-perceptible symptoms, and underlying code-level root causes. Our findings reveal that 68% of generated webpages exhibit at least one compatibility issue, underscoring the pervasive reliability concerns surrounding MLLM-generated front-end artifacts. The most prevalent symptoms are failures that disrupt the entire page layout (88.3%): pages shrink directly to fit the target screen with too small fonts, or exhibit scale mismatches that produce cut-off content. Failures localized to individual elements, such as image distortion or missing components, are comparatively less common (13.4%). Furthermore, although most MLLMs incorporate responsive design patterns into the generation, they fail to properly implement these codes.
  Guided by the findings, we develop XCompat, a lightweight offline compatibility issue detector that combines visual screenshots and the structural DOM tree for analysis. It achieves an F1 score of 0.903 on the WebCompat-test, outperforming the existing compatibility checking tools and LLM baselines. All datasets and tools are released to support future research on rendering reliability in MLLM-based front-end code generation.

</details>


### [4] [Memorization Diagnostics for Code LLMs Should be Scale-Aware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12771)
*Prateek Kumar Rajput,Abdoul Aziz Bonkoungou,Alberick Euraste Djiré,Xunzhu Tang,Yewei Song,Iyiola Emmanuel Olatunji,El Hacen Diallo,Jacques Klein,Tegawendé F. Bissyandé*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文探讨了大语言模型在代码任务中记忆与真实理解之间的界限。研究发现传统的探针技术在大型密集架构下严重失效，作者通过可逆数学变换将"表征负载"与"记忆"分离，揭示扩展后的编码器能吸收大量表征负载并收敛到正确的解决方案族。作者认为，与其纠结某个答案是否在训练时被记忆，更重要的是评估模型适应不同表面形式的能力，未来的评估应基于分离这两种现象的方法论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前文献频繁报道代码大模型存在广泛记忆，但传统探针技术（如同义词模糊、死代码注入等编码器式探针，以及依赖对数概率的解码器式探针）在扩展到大规模模型时性能严重退化。这种探针在小模型上有效却在大型模型上失效的异常现象，促使作者质疑将记忆与理解混为一谈的做法，从而驱动本研究去区分表征负载与记忆这两个不同现象。

Method: 作者对数值问题应用可逆数学变换，通过这种方式将"表征负载"（模型适应不同表面形式所需的能力）与"记忆"（训练期间见过特定解决方案）这两个因素进行分离和隔离。在变换后的问题上分别测量模型的表现，以区分模型是在吸收表征负载（展现泛化理解）还是依赖记忆。

Result: 研究结果显示，扩展后的编码器能够成功吸收大量的表征负载，同时仍然收敛到正确的解决方案族。这意味着即使答案在训练集中出现过（即存在记忆），模型所展现的适应不同表面形式的能力仍然真实有效。相比之下，传统探针在小模型上有效但在大型模型上失效，这一能力退化被证明是探针方法本身的问题而非模型缺陷。

Conclusion: 在实用的软件工程场景中，模型适应各种表面形式的能力（即泛化性）才是真正决定其可用性的关键因素，而非是否见过某个特定训练样本。虽然记忆会虚增受污染基准的分数，但当表征负载被剥离后，一个功能正确的答案是否源于记忆就不再重要。未来的评估必须围绕分离记忆与表征负载来设计，而不是使用将这些现象纠缠在一起的方法论，否则将无法准确衡量模型的真正能力。

Abstract: The extent to which large language models for code rely on memorization over genuine understanding remains highly debated. While current literature frequently reports widespread memorization, evaluating the underlying probing techniques across dense architectures reveals a severe breakdown in their utility at scale. Traditional encoder-style probes using perturbations such as synonym fuzzing or dead-code insertion struggle to expose memorization in scaled models, even on known-contaminated benchmarks, and decoder-style probes that rely on log probabilities show similar performance degradation. The specific mode of failure for these probes, particularly why such techniques disrupt smaller models but fail to impact larger ones, motivates us to untangle representation load from memorization rather than treating them as a single phenomenon. By applying invertible mathematical transforms to numeric problems, we isolate these two factors and reveal that scaled encoders successfully absorb substantial representation load while still converging on the correct family of solutions. In practical software engineering, this ability to adapt to varying surface forms is what truly matters for usability and generalizability in LLM and agentic applications. Whether a specific solution was seen during training becomes a much less pressing question because although memorization inflates scores on contaminated benchmarks, factoring out representation load makes it debatable how much we should truly care if a functional answer was originally memorized. Future evaluations must therefore be built around separating these phenomena rather than relying on methodologies that quietly entangle them.

</details>


### [5] [Dissecting Software Graphs: Structural Insights for Driver-Guided Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12859)
*Baihong Chen,Hua Ming,Weifeng Pan,Tian Xie,Haipeng Cai,Wen Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对多驱动（多模式）模糊测试展开实证研究，提出了基于静态调用图共享骨架和驱动动态覆盖投影的结构抽象方法，并设计了四阶段方法论。在27个OSS-Fuzz项目、43个可执行文件、854种驱动配置上验证，表明多驱动模糊测试比最佳单驱动基线在调用图节点覆盖上提升27.9%、CFG边覆盖提升73.5%，并发现了11个单驱动测试遗漏的独特bug。然而驱动贡献不均衡，子图在凝聚性、碎片化、模块化、重叠度上差异显著，残余未探索区域呈规律性分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管许多软件系统通过命令行选项、子命令和配置标志暴露多种执行模式，模糊测试的效果同时依赖变异输入和调用模式，但现有评估仍只关注覆盖率与bug数量，未系统厘清执行模式如何划分、重叠或遗漏软件结构，以及这些差异如何影响模糊测试的有效性。

Method: 提出一种结构抽象方法：以静态调用图作为共享骨干（backbone），将各驱动（driver）特定的动态覆盖率投影到骨干上，生成驱动诱导子图（driver-induced subgraphs）。基于该抽象，开发了四阶段方法论：骨干构建、模糊测试与性能剖析、基于图的分析、以及以研究问题驱动的评估。

Result: 在27个OSS-Fuzz派生C/C++项目（43个可执行文件、854种驱动配置）上，多驱动模糊测试在相同总预算下优于最佳单驱动基线：调用图节点覆盖提升27.9%，CFG边覆盖提升73.5%，并揭示11个单驱动测试难以发现的独特bug和异常行为。但驱动贡献不均，各驱动诱导子图在凝聚力、碎片化、模块化和重叠度上差异明显，残余未探索区域遵循反复出现的模式而非均匀长尾。

Conclusion: 多驱动模糊测试本质上是一个结构化探索问题。仅提升预算或简单组合驱动不足以充分覆盖软件结构，需要针对驱动贡献不均和子图结构差异进行更精细化的引导策略。

Abstract: Many software systems expose multiple execution modes through command-line options, subcommands, and configuration flags. For such programs, fuzzing depends on both mutated inputs and the invoked mode. Yet evaluations still focus on coverage and bug counts, leaving unclear how execution modes partition, overlap, and miss software structure, and how these differences affect effectiveness. We present an empirical study of software structure under multi-driver fuzzing. We propose a structural abstraction that uses a static call graph as a shared backbone and projects driver-specific dynamic coverage onto it to derive driver-induced subgraphs. Based on this abstraction, we develop a four-phase methodology for backbone construction, fuzzing and profiling, graph-based analysis, and research-question-driven evaluation. We apply it to 27 OSS-Fuzz-derived C/C++ projects, spanning 43 executables and 854 driver configurations. Under the same total budget, multi-driver fuzzing outperforms the best single-driver baseline, increasing covered call-graph nodes by 27.9% and CFG-edge coverage by 73.5%, and revealing 11 unique bugs and abnormal behaviors largely missed by single-driver fuzzing. However, driver contributions are uneven, subgraphs differ substantially in cohesion, fragmentation, modularity, overlap, and residual under-exploration follows recurring regimes rather than a homogeneous tail. These results show that multi-driver fuzzing is fundamentally a structural exploration problem.

</details>


### [6] [Requirements-Augmented Generation for Trustworthy Acceptance Testing of LLM-Based Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12970)
*Fanyu Wang,Chetan Arora,Zhenping Xie,Yonghui Liu,Kla Tantithamthavorn,Aldeida Aleti,Siwei Jiang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向LLM软件（LBS）的自动化验收测试框架，通过Requirements-Augmented Generation（REAG）生成上下文感知的测试预言，并结合置信度校准的级联判断机制来量化判定可靠性。在工业营养咨询应用上的案例研究显示，该框架实现了3.91/5的预言质量评分、98.8%的级联判定准确率以及31.7%的成本效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM软件（LBS）与传统软件不同，其行为具有上下文依赖性和随机性，导致经典的验收测试和测试预言方法不再适用。相同的查询可能因用户角色和软件上下文的不同而需要截然不同的响应，因此亟需一种能够自主解释用户指令、并在动态环境中可靠推断用户意图的自动化验收测试框架。

Method: 论文提出两项核心技术贡献：（1）Requirements-Augmented Generation（REAG），通过自适应RAG和自我推理检索相关软件需求、领域知识和用户角色，生成上下文感知的测试预言；（2）置信度校准的级联判定机制，通过模拟专家一致性来量化判定可靠性，对高置信度结果直接接受、模糊案例升级处理、不确定时弃权，并使用共形风险控制提供经验性可靠性保证。

Result: 在工业营养咨询应用上的案例研究表明：REAG达到3.91/5的预言质量评分，82%的案例达到合格或边缘预言质量；置信度校准级联判定实现98.8%的准确率，通过过滤不合格输出将预言质量从3.91提升至4.30，且相比单判定器基线实现了31.7%的成本效率提升。

Conclusion: 该框架通过生成上下文感知的测试预言和引入置信度校准的级联判定机制，有效解决了LBS验收测试中用户意图推断和判定可靠性的挑战，并通过工业案例验证了其实用性和经济可行性。

Abstract: LLM-based software (LBS) integrates large language models as core components to deliver flexible, personalised responses. Unlike traditional software with deterministic outputs, LBSs exhibit context-dependent, stochastic behaviour that renders classical acceptance testing and test oracles insufficient: the same query may require fundamentally different responses depending on user personas and software context. This gap creates an urgent need for automated acceptance testing frameworks that autonomously interpret user instructions, while reliably inferring user intentions in a changing environment. In this paper, we present an automated acceptance testing framework for LBS with calibrated verdict reliability via two technical contributions. First, we introduce Requirements-Augmented Generation (REAG), which interprets user intentions by retrieving relevant software requirements, domain knowledge, and personas via adaptive RAG and self-reasoning to generate context-aware test oracles. Second, recognising that oracle generation may retrieve irrelevant constraints, misinterpret intent, or hallucinate requirements, we introduce a confidence-calibrated cascade judgment. This method quantifies verdict reliability via simulated expert agreement -- accepting high-confidence verdicts, escalating ambiguous cases, or abstaining when uncertain -- with empirical reliability guarantees backed by conformal risk control. An industrial case study on a production nutrition advisory application demonstrates that REAG achieves a 3.91/5 oracle quality score, reaching qualified or marginal oracle quality in 82% of cases. The confidence-calibrated cascade achieves 98.8% accuracy, improves oracle quality from 3.91 to 4.30 by filtering unqualified outputs, and delivers a 31.7% cost-efficiency improvement over single-judge baselines, validating industrial viability

</details>


### [7] [How Powerful are LLMs in Generating Formal Program Specifications?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13077)
*Fanpeng Yang,Xing Li,Shuling Wang,Jie An,Zeyu Sun,Shenghua Feng,Wenhan Wang,Weiyi Wang,Naijun Zhan,Fanjiang Xu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Coins，一个基于Rocq的规范质量评估框架，通过在可信测试用例上实例化待评估的规范并生成具体证明义务来评估LLM生成的程序规范质量，避免了传统方法中证明难度与规范质量混淆的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化验证的采用受限于编写精确形式化规范的高成本。虽然LLM在定理证明和验证代码生成方面表现强劲，但其生成程序规范的能力尚不明确。现有评估方法要么需要验证实现一致性，要么需要证明规范之间的语义等价性，两者都极其困难且可能将证明难度与规范质量混为一谈。

Method: Coins框架基于Rocq，通过将待评估的规范实例化到可信测试用例上并生成具体的证明义务来评估规范质量。该设计利用了形式推理的不对称性：成功的证明提供可靠证据，而证明失败本身具有天然的歧义性。研究在HumanEval数据集上使用人工编写的Rocq规范集合进行了大规模研究。

Result: 实验结果表明：规范生成仍然是一个艰巨的挑战；验证复杂性会掩盖规范质量的真实差异；准确的规范评估（而非仅模型规模扩展）是理解LLM规范合成能力的关键；基于测试用例的形式推理提供了更忠实且更具区分度的进展度量。

Conclusion: 基于测试用例的形式化推理（如Coins框架）相比传统的实现一致性验证或语义等价性证明，能更可靠、更有区分度地评估LLM的程序规范生成能力，强调了改进评估方法论对于推动这一领域进展的重要性。

Abstract: Formal verification provides strong guarantees of software correctness, but its adoption is limited by the high cost of writing precise formal specifications. While recent large language models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities in theorem proving and verified code generation, their true ability to generate program specifications remains unclear. Existing evaluations require either verifying implementation conformance or proving semantic equivalence between specifications, both of which are formidably difficult and may conflate proof difficulty with specification quality. To address this problem, we introduce Coins, a Rocq based evaluation framework that assesses specification quality by instantiating specifications under evaluation on trusted test cases and generating concrete proof obligations. This design aligns with the asymmetric nature of formal reasoning, where successful proofs provide reliable evidence while proof failures are inherently ambiguous. Using Coins, we conduct a large scale study on HumanEval with a curated set of human written Rocq specifications. Our results show that specification generation remains a formidable challenge, and that verification complexity can obscure genuine differences in specification quality. Overall, we find that accurate specification evaluation, rather than model scaling alone, is central to understanding the power of LLMs for specification synthesis, and that test case based formal reasoning offers a more faithful and discriminative measure of progress.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化规格能提供强保证但手工编写成本高昂。现有的基于LLM的规格自动推断方法依赖白盒访问源码，这在工业场景中因知识产权风险和部署成本而难以落地。作者希望通过仅使用测试代码和动态执行轨迹（黑盒方式）来推断规格，绕过这些障碍。

Method: 该方法利用LLM仅从程序接口、选定输入及对应输出或状态变化中推断候选规格，实现内部细节完全隐藏。候选规格通过有界模型检查（bounded model checking）在本地验证，并将检查反馈用于迭代精化。

Result: 在SpecGenBench基准上的初步结果表明，测试能够引导LLM生成有意义的Java建模语言（JML）规格，同时揭示检查器兼容性和诊断反馈是可靠精化过程中的关键挑战。

Conclusion: 黑盒式LLM规格推断具有可行性，测试代码和动态执行轨迹足以引导LLM生成有意义的JML规格，但检查器兼容性和诊断反馈质量仍需进一步提升，以保证精化过程的可靠性。

Abstract: Formal specifications offer strong guarantees, but remain costly to write manually. Recent LLM-based approaches automate this by inferring specifications from source code, yet their reliance on white-box access poses barriers to industrial adoption due to intellectual property risks and deployment costs. Our approach uses LLMs to infer candidate specifications solely from test code and dynamic execution traces: the LLM observes only the program interface, selected inputs, and corresponding outputs or state changes, while the implementation internals remain hidden. Candidate specifications are validated locally using bounded model checking, with feedback guiding iterative refinement. Initial results on the SpecGenBench benchmark suggest that tests can guide LLMs towards meaningful Java Modeling Language specifications, while also highlighting checker compatibility and diagnostic feedback as key challenges for reliable refinement.

</details>

*Wenqiang Luo,Jacky Keung,Xiaoyu Shi,Yicheng Sun,Boyang Yang,Zhou Yang,Haoye Tian*

Main category: cs.SE

Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have advanced automatic program repair (APR) to the point where agentic systems routinely resolve real-world, repository-level issues. Yet the generated patch has received little scrutiny beyond whether it passes tests.
  In this paper, we identify patch verbosity as a major yet overlooked concern in LLM-based APR. Characterizing 28 state-of-the-art approaches on SWE-bench Verified, we find that even successful patches are consistently larger and more complex than developer patches, with the median approach producing 121.78% more total changes, 80.91% more net changes, and 43.99% higher cyclomatic complexity. We further show that this verbosity is rooted in capability-oriented design choices such as iterative refinement and broad context, and can hardly be reduced by surface-level controls such as output format or minimality prompts. Motivated by these findings, we formulate post-generation patch refinement and propose RECAP, a lightweight, plug-and-play adapter that attaches to existing repair frameworks after generation. RECAP's refiner is trained via supervised fine-tuning and direct preference optimization with distilled reasoning traces, on a dataset of patch pairs we construct from multiple sources. Across four host systems, prompting, commit-untangling, and minimality-aware baselines reduce patch size only by sacrificing 49 to 217 resolved instances. In contrast, RECAP achieves a substantially better size-correctness tradeoff, cutting average total changes from +242.14% to +4.24% and net changes from +348.24% to -39.75% relative to developer patches while preserving or improving resolution by up to 42 instances. Our results indicate that minimality cannot be simply reduced to syntactic compression, and that decoupling minimization from generation offers a practical path to more reviewable repairs.

</details>


### [10] [Integration-First Structural Coverage for Embedded Software:Trace-Based Evidence, Hybrid Runtime Analysis, and Cross-Variant Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13322)
*Alexander Weiss,Albert Schulz,Michael Wittner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向嵌入式项目的"集成优先"覆盖率策略，通过混合运行时分析（hRA）在优化编译（-O3）下利用嵌入式trace保留决策/条件边界的可观测性，并借助Hyper Coverage聚合层跨测试级别、构建配置合并证据，将"已覆盖"提升为"已覆盖或已证明合理"的完整性标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入式项目中结构覆盖率主要在单元级别收集（因插桩和可观测性成本低），导致覆盖率信号与最具代表性的完整性证据（集成/系统测试）错配。经典插桩会扰动时序、内存和并发行为，而纯trace重建覆盖率在编译器激进优化下会丢失决策和条件的可靠性。

Method: 从过程和设计两端解决：(1) 集成优先的覆盖率策略——以集成/系统测试为基线，通过显式闭环驱动残余差距；(2) 混合运行时分析（hRA）——最小化且保持语义的可观测性脚手架，使- O3优化构建中决策/条件边界在trace流中保持可区分，覆盖率状态和计数全部离目标端；(3) Hyper Coverage——跨测试级别、运行、变体和构建配置的证据合并层，暴露在所有变体中均未覆盖的源代码行。

Result: 该方法将对象到源码的映射从启发式重建转变为可审查的证据，使得分支、条件和MC/DC测量能够在类发布二进制上实际应用。

Conclusion: 通过hRA与trace路径的结合，覆盖率的完整性标准从单纯"已覆盖"演变为"已覆盖或已证明合理"，解决了嵌入式项目覆盖率信号与实际测试证据之间的错配问题。

Abstract: Structural coverage is widely used as evidence that testing is complete, yet in embedded projects it is predominantly collected at unit level, simply because that is where instrumentation and observability are inexpensive. This produces a mismatch. The most representative completeness signal would come from integration and system tests executed on the device under test, but classical instrumentation perturbs timing, memory footprint and concurrency behaviour, while purely trace-reconstructed coverage loses reliability for decisions and conditions as soon as the compiler optimizes aggressively. We address this mismatch from both ends. On the process side we describe an integrationfirst coverage strategy that treats integration and system tests as the baseline measurement and drives the residual gaps through an explicit closure loop, so that completeness is established as covered or justified rather than as covered alone. On the technical side we use embedded trace as the observation path and add hybrid runtime analysis (hRA): a minimal, semantics-preserving observability scaffolding that keeps decision and condition boundaries distinguishable in the trace stream of an optimized (-O3) build, while all coverage state and counting remain off-target. This converts object-to-source mapping from a heuristic reconstruction into reviewable evidence and makes branch, condition and MC/DC measurement practical on release-like binaries. Finally we describe Hyper Coverage, a consolidation layer that merges evidence across test levels, test runs, variants and build configurations, and that exposes source lines which remain untested in every relevant variant.

</details>


### [11] [Does Fixing Break Security? An Empirical Study of Security Degradation in Iterative LLM-Driven Infrastructure-as-Code Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13404)
*Benjamin Agyekum,Fabio Santos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究首次系统考察了迭代式LLM修复基础设施即代码(IaC)过程中的安全回归现象。基于IaC-Eval基准的5,968个场景、4,440次迭代转换,发现标准检测下13.8%的场景出现CIS安全回归,严格检测下降至3.3%。资源重构是主导根因(79.0%),回归转换伴随更高代码变动率和检查波动性。36.6%的回归可在平均1.2次迭代内自我纠正,迭代3次为最优停止点。研究为安全感知的反馈循环设计和迭代预算提供了经验依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有迭代LLM修复IaC的研究均采用累计最优(cumulative-best)指标评估,该指标在构造上单调非递减,掩盖了每次迭代后安全性的真实波动。此前从未有研究考察IaC迭代修复过程中的原始逐轮安全轨迹,即一次修复是否会在解决其他问题的同时引入新的安全缺陷。

Method: 从IaC-Eval基准选取5,968个场景,配置15种(6种模型特定RAG、9种模型聚合的非RAG、各3种温度),最多5次修复迭代,产生4,440次带Checkov双侧数据的迭代转换。追踪30个CIS检查ID,区分标准(包容性)和严格(仅排他性失败)两种检测模式,从代码差异分类根因,并统计分析回归率、代码变动量(Cohen's d)、检查波动性和自纠正模式。

Result: 标准检测下13.8%场景(24.8%转换)出现至少一次安全回归,严格检测降至3.3%场景(5.2%转换),表明多数表面回归是多资源测量伪影。资源重构是主导根因(79.0%)。回归转换的代码变动量为2.6倍(d=0.90),严格模式检查波动性为4.9倍(d=1.49)。36.6%的标准模式回归在平均1.2次迭代内自我纠正,迭代3为最优停止点。

Conclusion: 迭代式IaC修复确实会引入安全回归,但保守且可辩护的回归率约为场景的3.3%。研究结果支持安全感知的反馈循环设计,并提出可操作的迭代预算指导(建议最多3次迭代),为实践中的修复策略权衡提供了量化依据。

Abstract: Background: Iterative feedback loops are the dominant paradigm for improving LLM-generated Infrastructure-as-Code (IaC): validators such as Checkov and terraform validate feed error signals back for successive repair attempts. Prior work reports cumulative-best metrics, which are non-decreasing by construction, so the raw per-iteration security trajectory has never been examined for IaC. Aims: We study security regression (a previously-passing CIS Benchmark check that fails after a repair iteration) to determine whether and how often iterative LLM repair degrades security while fixing other issues. Method: We analyze 5,968 scenario timelines from the IaC-Eval benchmark, each one scenario run through one configuration for up to 5 repair iterations. The 15 configurations (six model-specific RAG, nine model-aggregated non-RAG, three temperatures each) yield 4,440 iteration transitions with Checkov data on both sides. We track 30 individual CIS check IDs and classify root causes from code diffs, under two detection modes: standard (inclusive) and strict (exclusive check failures only). Results: Under standard detection, 13.8% of scenarios (24.8% of transitions) exhibit at least one regression. Under strict detection the rate falls to 3.3% of scenarios (5.2% of transitions), indicating most apparent regressions are multi-resource measurement artifacts. Resource restructuring (79.0%) is the dominant root cause. Regression transitions show 2.6x more code churn (Cohen's d=0.90) and 4.9x higher strict-mode check volatility (d=1.49). Of standard-mode regressions, 36.6% self-correct within an average of 1.2 iterations; iteration 3 is the optimal stopping point. Conclusions: Iterative IaC repair does introduce security regressions, but the conservative, defensible rate is about 3.3% of scenarios. Our findings motivate security-aware feedback-loop design and actionable iteration-budget guidance.

</details>


### [12] [LLM-Assisted Dynamic Threat Analysis for Attacker-Reachable Software Weaknesses in Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13450)
*Md Wasiul Haque,Sagar Dasgupta,Mizanur Rahman,Md Rayhanur Rahman*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了LLM在自动为Autoware自动驾驶软件栈动态确认安全弱点方面的能力。尽管大型推理模型在静态分析辅助下首次编译成功率达64%，但构建集成（特别是依赖接线）成为主要瓶颈，所有37个崩溃均源自桩代码而非Autoware本身，在预算内未能动态确认任何候选弱点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统依赖大型安全关键软件栈，静态分析能识别候选弱点但难以动态确认可利用性，因为手动构建可执行测试工件非常困难。作者希望探究LLM能否自动化这一过程，以弥合静态分析与动态确认之间的鸿沟。

Method: 作者对Autoware的185个包进行编译器级静态分析，识别出1,375个决策规则、2,274个验证检查和482条输入到安全输出的数据流，据此构建弱点分类法并采样740个可达位点。使用两个本地开源权重LLM（推理模型和代码专用模型）、一个无静态上下文消融模型和一个朴素模板基线，生成3,700组测试工件，这些工件在真实构建系统下配合sanitizer编译，通过编译器反馈循环进行修复，可执行时进行模糊测试。

Result: 80%的首次编译失败源于依赖接线而非程序逻辑；推理模型首次编译成功率为64%，而代码专用模型仅6%；通过大量桩代码，只有推理模型达到完整对象可编译性，但不到一半的测试件到达模糊测试阶段；所有37个崩溃均来自桩代码而非Autoware；预算内未能动态确认任何候选弱点。

Conclusion: 构建集成（build integration）而非候选生成或模糊测试，是阻碍LLM辅助大型自动驾驶软件栈动态分析的主要瓶颈。该结论对依赖桩代码的LLM自动化测试方法的有效性提出质疑，表明当前LLM尚不足以独立完成全栈动态弱点确认，需要在依赖解析和构建环境集成方面取得突破。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles depend on large safety-critical software stacks, where weaknesses reachable from adversarial inputs may affect steering, braking, or other control decisions. Static analysis can identify candidate sites, but dynamically confirming exploitability requires executable test artifacts that are difficult to construct manually. We investigate whether large language models (LLMs) can automate this process for Autoware, an open-source autonomous-driving stack. We perform compiler-precise static analysis across 185 packages, identifying 1,375 decision rules, 2,274 validation checks, and 482 input-to-safety-output flows, from which we derive a weakness taxonomy and sample 740 reachable sites. Two local open-weight LLMs, a no-static-context ablation, and a naive-template baseline generate 3,700 artifact sets, which are compiled against the real build under sanitizers, repaired through compiler-in-the-loop feedback, and fuzzed when executable. The main result is a build-integration failure taxonomy showing that 80% of first-shot compilation failures arise from dependency wiring rather than program logic. The reasoning model compiled 64% of harnesses on the first attempt, compared with 6% for the code-specialized model. Repair achieved full object-compileability for the reasoning model only through extensive stubbing; fewer than half of its harnesses reached the fuzzer, and all 37 observed crashes originated in stubbed code rather than Autoware. No candidate weakness was dynamically confirmed within budget. These results show that build integration, not candidate generation or fuzzing, is the primary barrier to reliable LLM-assisted dynamic analysis of full autonomous-vehicle software stacks.

</details>


### [13] [CAPRI: Contract-Aware Proof Repair for Isabelle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13459)
*Jim Woodcock,Gabriel Leite,Augusto Sampaio,Ran Wei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了CAPRI，一个基于契约感知的Isabelle证明修复工作流。系统利用LLM生成候选修复，并以Isabelle验证证明正确性，同时用独立检查器执行机器可读的编辑契约，确保LLM只修改开发者授权的内容。在四个开发项目、12个失败证明、5个工作流的180次运行中，CAPRI产生了138个有效修复；在不应当修改受保护文本的场景下，全理论迭代工作流出现了6例违规，而证明体-only接口实现了零违规和29/36的有效修复率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型已被用于辅助发现Isabelle形式化证明，但现有的Isabelle构建只能验证提交的理论被接受，无法证明LLM仅修改了开发者授权的内容。这带来安全与审计方面的问题——需要一种机制确保LLM的编辑行为受合同约束且可追溯。

Method: CAPRI采用Isabelle验证证明正确性，并通过独立的检查器强制执行机器可读的编辑契约。系统保留提示、提议、候选仓库、诊断、判定结果和哈希以支持审计。作者在五个不同的工作流（包括一次性修复、迭代全理论编辑、证明体-only接口等）上进行了系统评估，并在后期进行了post hoc OpenRouter对比实验。

Result: 在180次运行中产生了138个有效修复。144个被Isabelle接受的终端候选中，有6个修改了受保护文本，全部发生在可编辑完整理论的迭代工作流中。证明体-only接口产生29/36有效修复且零违规；全理论对应工作流为31/36；一次性修复为22/36；后期冻结的迭代工作流为32/36。Sol配置配合匹配示范产生33/36修复，优于OpenAI Responses条件的29/36，但McNemar检验p=0.0625，差异未达统计显著。

Conclusion: CAPRI展示了LLM辅助Isabelle证明修复中引入编辑契约和审计追踪的价值。限制LLM的编辑范围（如证明体-only接口）能够有效消除受保护文本被修改的风险，同时维持较高的修复成功率。相关性能差异在统计上不显著，表明契约约束的强加并未显著牺牲修复能力。

Abstract: We address the use of large language models (LLMs) to help discover Isabelle proofs. An Isabelle build establishes that the submitted theory is accepted, but not that an LLM changed only what the developer authorised. We present CAPRI, a contract-aware repair workflow in which Isabelle checks the proof and an independent checker enforces a machine-readable edit contract. Prompts, proposals, candidate repositories, diagnostics, verdicts, and hashes are retained for audit. We evaluate five workflows on twelve failed proofs from four developments, with three replicates per task and condition, giving 180 runs and 138 valid repairs. Of 144 terminal candidates accepted by Isabelle, six had modified protected text; all arose in iterative workflows that could edit a complete theory. A proof-body-only interface produced 29/36 valid repairs and no contract violations, compared with 31/36 for the corresponding full-theory workflow. One-shot repair produced 22/36, while a later prospectively frozen iterative workflow produced 32/36; these figures compare complete workflows rather than individual mechanisms. A separate post hoc OpenRouter campaign found no improvement in the designated Luna comparisons. A Sol configuration with matched demonstrations produced 33/36 repairs, compared with 29/36 in the frozen OpenAI Responses condition, but the difference was not statistically significant in a one-sided exact McNemar test ($p=0.0625$).

</details>

### [14] [Test-Time Optimization of Query Embeddings with Ranking Aware Reward Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12569)
*Tianyu Chen,Jiaxing Wu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TTT-Embed框架通过将排序奖励蒸馏为冻结模型输出嵌入空间中的轻量级学习向量，实现无需参数访问的测试时检索优化，在15个MTEB检索任务上最高提升+8.36 nDCG@10，并支持奖励的跨查询和跨任务复用，同时解决灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集检索器依赖冻结编码器与预计算索引的向量相似度排序，虽然测试时使用重排器或LLM评审器的排序奖励可以提升结果，但现有方法在单个查询后即丢弃此信号。若更新检索器权重以复用奖励，则需要访问模型参数（闭源模型不可用）且计算代价高昂。因此需要一种无需参数访问、计算开销小的方式将排序奖励转化为可复用的信号。

Method: TTT-Embed提出在冻结模型的输出嵌入空间中优化一个轻量级学习向量，仅从检索器自身候选文档的标量排序分数中蒸馏奖励信号。该方法无需模型权重访问、无需真实标签、无需修改索引。引入单一作用域参数控制奖励的复用粒度（全局、任务、查询），在固定奖励计算预算下实现可复用性与特异性的原则性权衡。

Result: 在5个嵌入模型和15个MTEB检索任务上，TTT-Embed将测试时检索性能最高提升+8.36 nDCG@10。学习到的状态可有效泛化到未见查询（最高+8.57 nDCG@10）和未见任务（最高+4.71 nDCG@10）。此外，由于基座权重完全冻结，成功解决了灾难性遗忘问题，恢复通用能力最高+8.00 nDCG@10，甚至超过原始基座模型，同时保留领域内特化能力。

Conclusion: 排序奖励可以作为一种可复用的测试时状态，通过TTT-Embed在冻结嵌入空间中实现高效的奖励蒸馏与复用。该方法为任何嵌入模型（包括闭源API）提供了预算高效的适应性优化途径，并揭示了奖励预算扩展时最优共享粒度从全局到任务再到查询的动态转移规律。

Abstract: Dense retrievers rank documents using vector similarity between a frozen encoder and a precomputed index. While test-time ranking rewards from a reranker or LLM judge can improve results, existing methods discard this signal after a single query. Updating the retriever's weights makes rewards reusable, but this requires parameter access, which is unavailable for closed-source models, and is computationally prohibitive. We propose TTT-Embed (Test-Time Tuning of Embeddings), a framework that distills ranking rewards into a lightweight, learned vector within the output embedding space of a frozen model. This vector is optimized purely from scalar ranking scores assigned to the retriever's own candidate documents, requiring no access to model weights, ground-truth labels, or modifications to index. A single scope parameter controls rewards reuse (global, task, or query), enabling a principled trade-off between reusability and specificity under a fixed reward computation budget. We demonstrate that as the available reward budget scales, the optimal sharing scope shifts dynamically from global-wise to task-wise and finally to query-wise. Evaluated across five embedding models and 15 MTEB retrieval tasks, TTT-Embed improves test-time retrieval by up to +8.36 nDCG@10. Crucially, the learned states generalize effectively to unseen queries (up to +8.57 nDCG@10) and unseen tasks (up to +4.71 nDCG@10). Furthermore, TTT-Embed successfully resolves catastrophic forgetting: by leaving base weights entirely frozen, it recovers degraded general capabilities (up to +8.00 nDCG@10, even surpassing the original base model) while preserving in-domain specialization. These results establish ranking rewards as a reusable test-time state, enabling budget-efficient adaptation for any embedding model, including closed-source APIs.

</details>


### [15] [Knowledge Synthesis Review Framework: Task-Level Benchmarking of LLM-Based Systems for Multi-Source Evidence Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12741)
*Wafa Shafqat,Mark Patterson,Steven N. Liss*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出KSR（知识综合综述）框架，一种人机协同的证据综合方法，将综述流程分解为筛选、提取、分析和综合四个任务，分别用基准测试评估四种LLM系统（GPT-5、Claude Sonnet 4、Gemini 2.5 Pro、NotebookLM）在各任务上的表现，并路由至最优系统以持续人工验证。在244篇文档的基准子集上评估发现，没有单一系统在所有任务上全面领先，跨源综合和解释性分析仍需专家判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速演变领域的证据分散在不同质量的学术研究、行业报告、政策文件和媒体来源中，传统综述方法难以跟上更新速度。LLM可能加速这一工作，但其在综述所需的多个不同认知任务上的可靠性尚未得到系统评估，需要一种能明确各任务表现并保持人类问责制的框架。

Method: KSR框架将综述分解为筛选（screening）、提取（extraction）、分析（analysis）和综合（synthesis）四阶段。构建了一个来自1,893篇文档语料库的244篇文档基准子集（涵盖AI与工作主题，四种来源类型），以高信度黄金标准（92.2%一致性，kappa=0.80）评估GPT-5、Claude Sonnet 4、Gemini 2.5 Pro和NotebookLM在各任务上的表现，并采用截止后文档进行污染检查。

Result: Claude Sonnet 4在筛选准确率上最高（82.8%），GPT-5召回率最高（91.8%）但特异性较低；提取在标题和来源字段超过90%一致性，但作者和参考文献字段表现下降；在解释性分析和跨源综合中性能下降最显著。污染检查未发现先前暴露导致的结果膨胀。将路由工作流应用于完整语料库后，发现了单源综述会遗漏的跨源不对称和盲点（如工人福祉、小企业和全球南方）。

Conclusion: KSR提供了透明、可审计、模型无关的框架来治理LLM辅助研究综述，同时保持人类问责制。核心结论是：没有单一LLM在所有综述任务上全面占优，任务路由结合持续专家验证是推进LLM辅助证据综合的可行路径，解释性分析和跨源综合仍需人工判断。

Abstract: Evidence in rapidly evolving fields is fragmented across academic studies, industry reports, policy documents, and media sources that differ in quality, structure, and purpose, making timely synthesis difficult. Large language models (LLMs) may accelerate this work, but their reliability across the distinct cognitive tasks of a review remains uncertain. We introduce the Knowledge Synthesis Review (KSR), a human-in-the-loop framework that decomposes evidence synthesis into screening, extraction, analysis, and synthesis, benchmarks LLM-based systems on each task against expert reference standards, and routes each task to the best-performing system under continuous expert validation. We evaluated GPT-5, Claude Sonnet 4, Gemini 2.5 Pro, and NotebookLM on a 244-document benchmark subset drawn from a 1,893-document corpus on AI and work spanning four source types, against a gold standard with high inter-rater reliability (92.2% agreement, kappa = 0.80). No system led on all tasks. Claude Sonnet 4 achieved the highest screening accuracy (82.8%) and GPT-5 the highest recall (91.8%) at the expense of lower specificity. Extraction exceeded 90% agreement for titles and sources but degraded in author and reference fields. Performance declined most in interpretive analysis and cross-source synthesis, where expert judgment remained essential. A contamination check on post-cutoff documents showed no evidence that prior exposure inflated results. Applied to the full corpus, the routed workflow surfaced cross-source asymmetries and blind spots that single-source synthesis would miss, including worker well-being, small firms, and the Global South. KSR offers a transparent, auditable, model-agnostic framework for governing LLM assistance in research synthesis while preserving human accountability.

</details>


### [16] [DrEM: Dual-Side Robust Ensemble Ranking from Noisy User Preference Predictions in Video Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12778)
*Canwei Huang,Tiantian He,Xiaoxiao Xu,Jun Zhang,Ziran Deng,Weike Pan,Chunjie Chen,Kaiqiao Zhan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出DrEM框架，解决工业视频推荐系统中上游多任务模型输出（pxtrs）携带预测噪声导致集成排序模型在监督侧和特征侧失效的问题，通过风险去噪鲁棒损失和偏好保持排序一致性正则化，实现双侧重稳健的集成排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业视频推荐系统采用多阶段架构，集成排序阶段融合上游多任务模型的pxtrs预测。然而pxtrs作为上游模型输出不可避免地包含预测噪声，这些噪声在监督侧（可能导致代理偏好翻转并引入错误梯度）和特征侧（噪声通过模型输入传播并破坏排序分数稳定性）都造成负面影响。现有方法将pxtrs视为可靠信号，忽视了这一预测噪声问题。

Method: DrEM包含两个核心组件：1）风险去噪鲁棒损失（risk-denoising robust loss），通过估计偏好翻转概率来校正经验风险；2）偏好保持排序一致性正则化（preference-preserving ranking consistency regularizer），从预测噪声分布中采样扰动以提升特征侧输出稳定性。理论上还推导了预测噪声的近似分布，并证明在翻转概率估计误差下鲁棒损失仍具优势。

Result: 通过大量离线实验和大规模在线A/B测试验证了DrEM的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DrEM首次从双侧重（监督侧和特征侧）的角度系统性解决集成排序中的pxtrs预测噪声问题，理论分析与实验共同证明了其有效性与实用性。

Abstract: Industrial video recommendation systems typically adopt a multi-stage architecture. At the ensemble ranking stage, multi-dimensional user preference predictions (pxtrs) from an upstream multi-task model are fused into a unified ranking score to reflect user satisfaction. Since users' true satisfaction is difficult to observe directly, ensemble ranking models commonly use pxtrs both as input features and as a source for constructing proxy preferences. However, as outputs of an upstream prediction model, pxtrs inevitably contain prediction noise, which propagates to downstream learning across two sides. On the supervision side, noisy pxtrs may flip proxy preferences and introduce erroneous gradients. On the feature side, pxtr noise may propagate through model inputs and destabilize ranking scores. Existing ensemble ranking methods typically treat pxtrs as reliable signals and overlook such prediction noise. To address this, we propose DrEM, a dual-side robust ensemble ranking framework. Our DrEM introduces a risk-denoising robust loss that corrects the empirical risk using estimated preference flip probability. Meanwhile, it samples perturbations from the distribution of prediction noise and introduces a preference-preserving ranking consistency regularizer to improve feature-side output stability. Theoretically, we obtain an approximate distribution of the prediction noise and prove that the robust loss remains superior under flip probability estimation error. Extensive offline experiments and large-scale online A/B tests demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of our DrEM.

</details>


### [17] [A Comprehensive Empirical Evaluation of Vector Database Systems for Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search: Performance, Quality, and Resource Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12812)
*Ashen Rashmiks,Tiroshan Madushanka*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文对七个主流向量数据库系统（FAISS、Qdrant、Milvus、Weaviate、Chroma、pgvector和LanceDB）进行了系统性的实证评估，覆盖六个数据集、超过400万向量、15项指标，并发布了开源基准测试框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管向量数据库在RAG、语义搜索和推荐系统等领域日益重要，但缺乏全面、可复现的统一基准来同时评估检索质量、查询延迟、吞吐量和资源利用率，导致从业者难以做出明智的系统选型决策。

Method: 选取七个代表性向量数据库系统，在六个跨越经典视觉描述符（SIFT、GIST）到基于Transformer的文本嵌入（MS MARCO、GloVe）的数据集上进行测试，涵盖96至960维度、超过400万向量；测量15项指标，包括检索质量（Recall@K、Precision@K、MRR、NDCG@K、Hit Rate@K）、查询性能（延迟百分位、QPS、冷启动延迟）和资源消耗（索引构建时间、内存、存储）。

Result: FAISS在SIFT1M上实现最高单节点吞吐量（866 QPS）但缺乏数据库运维功能；Weaviate开箱即用召回率最优（>99%）；Qdrant在完整数据库中延迟最佳（中位数4.55ms）；LanceDB以牺牲检索质量换取显著更快的索引构建速度。

Conclusion: 不同向量数据库系统在检索质量、性能和功能之间各有取舍，不存在单一最优系统；作者据此推导出面向从业者的系统选型指南，并将基准测试框架开源发布。

Abstract: Vector databases have emerged as critical infrastructure for modern artificial intelligence applications, particularly retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), semantic search, and recommendation systems. Despite their growing importance, there remains a significant gap in comprehensive, reproducible benchmarks that jointly evaluate retrieval quality, query latency, throughput, and resource utilization. We present a systematic empirical evaluation of seven prominent vector database systems: FAISS, Qdrant, Milvus, Weaviate, Chroma, pgvector, and LanceDB. Our methodology spans six diverse datasets, from classical computer-vision descriptors (SIFT, GIST) to transformer-based text embeddings (MS MARCO, GloVe), encompassing over 4 million vectors at dimensionalities from 96 to 960. We measure 15 metrics spanning retrieval quality (Recall@K, Precision@K, MRR, NDCG@K, Hit Rate@K), query performance (latency percentiles, QPS, cold-start latency), and resource consumption (index build time, memory, storage). On SIFT1M, FAISS achieves the highest single-node throughput (866 QPS) but lacks database operational features; Weaviate provides the best out-of-the-box recall (> 99%); Qdrant offers the best latency among full databases (4.55~ms median); and LanceDB trades retrieval quality for substantially faster index construction. We derive system-selection guidelines for practitioners and release our benchmarking framework as open-source software.

</details>


### [18] [Query Translation vs. Cross-Lingual Embeddings for Sinhala-Tamil E-Government Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12820)
*Dharshi Balasubramaniyam,Tiroshan Madushanka*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文对跨语言信息检索（CLIR）方法进行了比较评估，针对使用僧伽罗语和泰米尔语查询检索英文政府信息。结果显示，跨语言嵌入（CLE）方法，特别是BGE-M3模型，在Recall@15上分别达到96.2%（僧伽罗语-英语）和95.6%（泰米尔语-英语），优于最佳查询翻译方法（Google Translate：92.4%和93.0%），且避免了翻译开销，证明多语言嵌入模型是低资源政府领域跨语言RAG的更有效和可扩展的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言环境下的政府信息检索面临严峻挑战，僧伽罗语和泰米尔语查询检索英文政府信息需要有效的跨语言检索方法。现有研究缺乏对这些方法在真实政府领域基准上的系统比较，因此需要一个人类验证的基准来评估不同CLIR范式的实际表现。

Method: 论文构建了一个包含500个僧伽罗语、泰米尔语和英语问答对的人类验证基准，源于斯里兰卡政府信息中心（GIC）的1,699个分段上下文。比较了两种CLIR范式：(1) 查询翻译（QT）方法，使用Google Translate、NLLB和mBART50；(2) 跨语言嵌入（CLE）方法，使用LaBSE、多语言E5和BGE-M3，并以单语英语检索作为基线。性能通过Recall@k（k=1,3,5,10,15）进行评估。

Result: 单语检索表现较差（Recall@15<10%），而所有CLIR方法均显著提升检索精度。BGE-M3取得最高性能，Recall@15在僧伽罗语-英语中达96.2%，泰米尔语-英语中达95.6%，优于最佳查询翻译方法（Google Translate：92.4%和93.0%），且无需翻译开销。

Conclusion: 多语言嵌入模型（特别是BGE-M3）为低资源政府领域的跨语言检索增强生成（RAG）提供了比查询翻译更有效且可扩展的解决方案，在提升检索精度的同时避免了翻译环节的计算和延迟开销。

Abstract: This paper presents a comparative evaluation of cross-lingual information retrieval (CLIR) methods for retrieving English government information using Sinhala and Tamil queries. Two CLIR paradigms are investigated: Query Translation (QT), employing Google Translate, NLLB, and mBART50, and Cross-Lingual Embeddings (CLE), using LaBSE, multilingual E5, and BGE-M3, with monolingual English retrieval as the baseline. Experiments are conducted on a human-verified benchmark comprising 500 Sinhala, Tamil, and English question-answer pairs derived from 1,699 segmented contexts from Sri Lanka's Government Information Center (GIC). Retrieval performance is evaluated using Recall@k (k = 1, 3, 5, 10, 15). Monolingual retrieval performs poorly (Recall@15 <10%), whereas all CLIR approaches substantially improve retrieval accuracy. Among them, BGE-M3 achieves the highest Recall@15, reaching 96.2% for Sinhala-English and 95.6% for Tamil-English, outperforming the best QT approach (Google Translate: 92.4% and 93.0%) while avoiding translation overhead. These results demonstrate that multilingual embedding models provide a more effective and scalable solution for cross-lingual retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) in low-resource government domains.

</details>


### [19] [FSGR: Mitigating Token Frequency Bias for Fair SID-Based Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12845)
*Yuchen Zheng,Sihan Xu,Jingwen Yang,Xiangrui Cai,Haiwei Zhang,Xiaojie Yuan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出FSGR框架，首次识别并解决SID（语义ID）生成式推荐中的Token频率偏差问题——高频SID token被系统性过度预测而低频token被欠预测，导致不同类别物品曝光不公平。FSGR通过在SID构建阶段引入OT分配优化和双准则重锚机制，以及在推荐训练阶段采用两阶段训练和层级频率校准，在三个公开数据集上实现平均Gini公平性提升超20%的同时保持推荐精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SID生成式推荐近年来表现优异，但现有方法存在被忽视的公平性问题——Token频率偏差。该偏差源于SID构建时语义码本不均衡与训练时流行度偏差及MLE目标的共同作用，造成物品类别间不公平曝光。现有SID方法只关注码本质量，LLM去偏方法因SID token的层级语义特性而直接应用效果不佳。

Method: FSGR框架包含两个阶段：(1) SID构建阶段：采用基于最优传输（OT）的分配优化和双准则重锚机制，构建更均衡的SID表示空间；(2) 推荐训练阶段：采用两阶段训练策略，引入层级频率校准（Hierarchical Frequency Calibration）进行逐层公平性微调。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上使用三种骨干模型进行实验，FSGR有效缓解了token频率偏差，平均Gini公平性提升超过20%，同时保持具有竞争力的推荐准确度。

Conclusion: FSGR通过同时从SID构建和推荐训练两个环节入手，系统性地缓解了SID生成式推荐中的token频率偏差，在提升公平性与保持推荐性能之间取得了良好平衡，为SID-based生成式推荐的公平性问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: Semantic ID (SID)-based generative recommendation has recently achieved remarkable success. However, existing methods suffer from a previously overlooked fairness issue, which we term \textbf{Token Frequency Bias}, where high-frequency SID tokens are systematically over-predicted while low-frequency SID tokens are under-predicted. This bias originates from the combined effects of imbalanced semantic codebooks during SID construction, and popularity bias together with the maximum likelihood estimation objective during recommendation training, resulting in unfair exposure across item categories. Existing SID methods mainly focus on improving codebook quality and overlook the impact of token frequency imbalance on downstream recommendation fairness, while LLM debiasing methods often yield suboptimal results when directly applied to SID-based recommendation, due to the hierarchical semantics of SID tokens. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{FSGR}, a fairness optimization framework for SID-based generative recommendation. During SID construction, FSGR employs OT-based Assignment Optimization and Dual-Criteria Re-anchor mechanism to form a more balanced SID representation space. During recommendation training, it adopts a two-stage training strategy and introduces Hierarchical Frequency Calibration for layer-specific fairness fine-tuning. Experiments on three public datasets with three backbone models demonstrate that FSGR mitigates token frequency bias and delivers an average Gini fairness improvement of over 20\% while maintaining competitive recommendation accuracy.

</details>


### [20] [STAR: Structured Tokenization and Target-Aware Interest Representation for PCVR Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12986)
*Yimeng Xu,Haorui Zhang,Yingqi Song,Ying Jiang,Lan Ma*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出STAR框架，用于KDD Cup 2026腾讯UniRec挑战赛中的点击后转化率（PCVR）预测。该框架结合结构化特征分词与目标感知兴趣表征，基于HyFormer风格的多序列骨干网络，引入高基数信号恢复、显式用户-物品交互分词、目标感知序列解码及InfoNCE启发的加权用户-物品对比辅助目标，并对训练与推理管线进行对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代工业推荐系统的PCVR预测任务面临多重挑战：需联合建模异构非序列特征、多行为用户序列和目标物品感知的用户兴趣，同时要应对高基数稀疏特征、缺失值以及训练-推理不一致等问题，亟需一个实用且鲁棒的解决方案。

Method: STAR框架包含四个核心组件：(1) 结构化特征分词（Structured Tokenization）与高基数信号恢复；(2) 显式用户-物品交互分词；(3) 目标感知序列解码（Target-Aware Sequence Decoding）；(4) 基于InfoNCE的加权用户-物品对比辅助目标。同时通过重建特征映射表和结构超参数来对齐训练与推理管线。

Result: 实验表明时间上下文带来最大增益，对比对齐、目标感知兴趣编码和高基数序列特征恢复也有较小但有益的贡献。主要消融实验验证了各组件对排序AUC的提升效果，LogLoss作为校准诊断指标。

Conclusion: STAR框架在PCVR预测任务上通过结构化分词与目标感知兴趣表征的组合，在不同组件贡献上具有可解释性，为工业推荐系统中的转化率预测提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Post-click conversion rate (PCVR) prediction is a core ranking task in industrial recommender systems. Modern ranking models must jointly capture heterogeneous non-sequential features, multi-behavior user sequences, and target-item-aware user interests, while remaining robust to high-cardinality sparse features, missing values, and train-inference inconsistencies. In this paper, we present STAR (Structured Tokenization and Target-Aware Interest Representation), a practical framework for the KDD Cup 2026 Tencent UniRec Challenge. STAR combines structured feature tokenization with target-aware interest representation on top of a HyFormer-style multi-sequence backbone. It introduces high-cardinality signal recovery, explicit user-item interaction tokens, target-aware sequence decoding, and a weighted user-item contrastive auxiliary objective inspired by InfoNCE. We further align the training and inference pipelines by reconstructing feature remapping tables and structural hyperparameters from the saved training configuration. Experiments on the challenge dataset identify the components that most reliably improve ranking AUC, while LogLoss is reported as a calibration diagnostic. The main ablation study shows a large gain from temporal context, with smaller but useful contributions from contrastive alignment, target-aware interest encoding, and high-cardinality sequence feature recovery.

</details>


### [21] [Generative Universal Multimodal Retrieval with Dual-role Identifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12987)
*Kaipeng Li,Haitao Yu,Xuanchen Zhou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出DrIG，一个基于双重角色标识符的通用多模态生成式检索框架，通过将残差量化标识符同时用作自回归解码的序列角色和无序集合角色，统一支持文本、图像及混合模态的检索任务，在M-BEIR基准和文生图评测上优于现有最优生成式多模态检索方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式信息检索（GIR）虽作为传统"索引-检索-重排"管道的替代方案而兴起，但仍面临三大挑战：(1)受限的从左到右解码易受前缀级错误和局部最优影响；(2)大多数现有GIR研究局限于单模态，跨文本、图像及图文混合项的多模态指令感知检索未得到充分探索；(3)基于离散标识符的GIR虽效率更高，但检索精度仍落后于先进稠密向量检索方法。

Method: DrIG为每个候选赋予单一的残差量化标识符，该标识符承担两种互补角色：在序列角色中，标识符被自回归解码，首token显式建模模态信息，其余token逐步捕获更细粒度的语义；在集合角色中，同一组token被重新解释为无序集合，提供与前缀无关的相关性先验，用于引导受限束搜索并缓解局部最优错误。

Result: 在M-BEIR基准和文生图评测数据集上的广泛实验表明：(1)DrIG在各类任务上一致优于现有最优生成式多模态基线，且混合重排在效率-效果权衡上优于强稠密检索器；(2)消融实验和扩展性分析揭示了基础LMM、束大小、重排深度及融合策略对检索性能的影响，为系统设计提供了实用指导。

Conclusion: DrIG通过双重角色标识符设计，同时解决了GIR在前缀级错误、多模态泛化和精度-效率权衡方面的开放问题，为通用多模态生成式检索提供了新范式，并通过消融分析为实际系统部署提供了可操作的调优指导。

Abstract: Generative information retrieval (GIR) has emerged as a compelling alternative to the conventional index-retrieve-then-rank retrieval pipeline by training a generator to produce the identifiers of relevant items directly. Despite its promise, a number of open challenges still remain. First, constrained left-to-right decoding is vulnerable to prefix-level errors and local optima. Second, most prior GIR research remains largely unimodal, leaving instruction-aware retrieval across text, image, and mixed image-text items underexplored. Third, although discrete identifier-based GIR offers higher efficiency, its retrieval accuracy still lags behind that of the cutting-edge dense-vector-based retrieval methods. Motivated by these challenges, we propose DrIG, a novel Generative framework for universal multimodal retrieval featuring Dual-role Identifiers, which supports diverse retrieval tasks across multiple modalities and domains. Each candidate is assigned a single residual-quantized identifier that serves two complementary roles. In its sequential role, the identifier is decoded autoregressively, where the first token explicitly models modality and the remaining tokens capture progressively finer semantics. In its set-based role, the same tokens are reinterpreted as an unordered set to provide a prefix-independent relevance prior, which guides constrained beam search and alleviates local-optimum errors. Extensive experiments on the M-BEIR benchmark and the text-to-image evaluation datasets show that:(1)DrIG consistently outperforms state-of-the-art generative multimodal baselines across diverse tasks, while hybrid reranking achieves a favorable efficiency-effectiveness trade-off against strong dense retrievers. (2)Ablation and scaling analyses reveal how the base LMM, beam size, reranking depth, and fusion strategy affect retrieval performance, providing practical guidance for system design.

</details>


### [22] [When Should Multi-Round RAG Stop? Structured Stopping Judgments and Retrieval Reduction in Search-R1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13237)
*Weimeng Luo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文在冻结的Search-R1流水线上训练了一个S2G风格的2B参数judge模型，用于多轮RAG的停止决策。该方法在900个HotpotQA问题上训练了3,009个状态，最终在确认测试集上将检索调用减少77次（3.70%），而Official Exact Match仅下降0.625个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮RAG需要决定何时停止检索，但现有方法将停止决策视为独立的状态分类任务。由于部署策略由每条轨迹上第一个STOP决定，这本质上是一个序列选择问题，而非独立分类问题，需要针对性地设计停止判断机制。

Method: 将S2G-RAG的结构化充分性-差距（sufficiency-and-gap）判断机制适配到冻结的Search-R1流水线上。训练一个Qwen3.5-2B judge模型，基于3,009个来自900个互不重叠HotpotQA问题的状态样本进行训练。Search-R1的reasoner、retriever、corpus、prompt和search budget均保持不变，仅替换judge checkpoint并通过分组验证选择停止阈值，之后再冻结进行确认性评估。

Result: 在确认测试集上，该策略相比原生Search-R1将检索调用减少了77次（3.70%），但Official Exact Match下降了0.625个百分点。结果表明结构化judge减少了检索量，同时大致保持了答案准确率。

Conclusion: 训练的S2G风格结构化judge能够在减少检索量的同时大致保持答案准确率，但作者明确强调结果并不意味着准确率不变或提升、也不意味着安全停止或总推理成本降低。

Abstract: Multi-round retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) must decide when to stop searching as evidence accumulates. Because the deployed policy is determined by the first STOP on each trajectory, this is a sequential selection problem rather than an independent state-classification task. We adapt S2G-RAG's structured sufficiency-and-gap judgment to a frozen Search-R1 pipeline and train a Qwen3.5-2B judge on 3,009 states from 900 disjoint HotpotQA questions. Search-R1's reasoner, retriever, corpus, prompt, and search budget remain unchanged, while the judge checkpoint and stopping threshold are selected on grouped validation and frozen before confirmatory evaluation. On the confirmatory test set, the resulting policy reduces retrieval calls by 77 (3.70\%) relative to Native Search-R1, while Official Exact Match decreases by 0.625 percentage points. Thus, the trained S2G-style structured judge reduces retrieval while broadly preserving answer accuracy. The result does not imply unchanged or improved accuracy, safe stopping, or lower total inference cost.

</details>


### [23] [Structure then Query: Enabling Precise Analytical Queries over Unstructured Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13384)
*Teng Lin,Yuyu Luo,Nan Tang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: AnnoIndex是一个基于注释索引和结构化查询引擎的文档分析系统，通过SchemaLoop自动构建层级化注释模式并使用轻量级语言模型提取属性值，将非结构化文本物化为结构化索引，同时提出SQL扩展的执行计划编译方法，按成本升序逐步执行提取操作，实验显示其在三个真实数据集上平均F1达到0.87，优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非结构化文档占企业和网络数据的大多数，尽管LLM发展迅速，但主流检索仍依赖基于向量相似度的模糊匹配，难以准确获取信息并进行结构化分析和推理。现有方法无法将文档像数据库一样进行精确操作，缺乏低成本、可解释的信息提取和查询机制。

Method: AnnoIndex提出两个核心组件：(1) 注释索引（Annotation Index），通过SchemaLoop模块从原始语料自动创建层级化注释模式，使用轻量级语言模型提取特定属性值，将非结构化文本物化为结构化索引，支持低成本过滤和查询，并将属性抽取成本从在线查询摊销到一次性构建；(2) 结构化查询引擎（Structured Query Engine），将用户问题编译为基于SQL扩展的执行计划，先利用注释索引精确过滤文档，再按成本升序逐步执行提取操作，仅对需要深度语义理解的极少数语料调用LLM，提取结果合并回注释索引以降低后续查询成本。

Result: 在三个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，AnnoIndex在所有评估指标上持续优于最先进的基线方法，达到最高平均F1分数0.87，同时在复杂的多跳连接和渐进式推理查询上表现稳健。

Conclusion: AnnoIndex通过将文档结构化索引化并按成本分层执行查询，有效解决了非结构化文档上的精准信息检索与结构化推理问题，证明了将非结构化文本向数据库式操作转变的可行性，为大规模文档分析提供了高效且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Unstructured documents constitute the majority of enterprise and web data. With the rapid development of large language models(LLMs), researchers have started to build data systems that analyze unstructured textual documents like operating on databases. However, because mainstream retrieval methods still relies on fuzzy matching based on vector similarity, accurately obtaining information and performing structured analysis and reasoning remains a major challenge. To address these limitations, AnnoIndex introduces two core fundamental components. The first is Annotation Index. The system uses a module called SchemaLoop to automatically create hierarchical annotation schemas from the raw corpus, and then uses lightweight language model to extract specific values. It turns scattered unstructured text into a materialized, structured index that enables low-cost filtering and querying. The annotation index avoids the black-box matching of vector similarity and amortizes attribute extraction costs from online queries to a one-time build. The second innovation is a Structured Query Engine. It compiles user questions into execution plans based on SQL extension. It first uses the Annotation Index for precise documents filtering, then gradually applies extraction operations in ascending order of cost, resorting to LLMs only for the remaining minimal fraction of the corpus that require deep semantic understanding. The extracted attributions are merged into the annotation index, reducing the cost of future queries. Experiments on three real-world datasets demonstrate that AnnoIndex consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving the highest average F1 score (0.87) while maintaining robust performance on complex multi-hop join and progressive reasoning queries.

</details>

### [24] [Position: Reasoning is a Learnable Rule-Based Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12325)
*Rachel Lawrence,Jacqueline Maasch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出当前生成式AI社区对"推理"缺乏可操作的统一定义，导致推理评测的构念效度无法验证，进而阻碍了可信自主推理的量化进展。作者基于文献综合给出了推理的操作性定义，并提供了AI推理研究沟通的最佳实践清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管自主推理是AI领域最具科学和经济价值的话题，且深度概率生成模型已取得大量进展，但生成式AI社区尚未在推理的操作性定义上达成共识，且常隐性排斥逻辑与可验证自动推理领域对推理的传统处理方式。这种定义上的模糊性使得推理评估的构念效度（construct validity）不可验证，削弱了向可信自主推理方向可量化进步的基础。

Method: 本文属于立场论文（position paper），方法主要包括：(1) 综合文献提出操作性定义，将有效且健全的推理定位为一种可学习的、基于规则的过程（learnable rule-based process）；(2) 制定一份用于AI推理研究沟通的最佳实践检查清单（checklist）。

Result: 提供了两项核心产出：其一，基于多领域文献综合的操作性推理定义，强调推理是结合了有效性（validity）与健全性（soundness）的可学习规则化过程；其二，一套规范AI推理研究报告中沟通与评估的最佳实践清单，用以增强研究的可复现性与可比性。

Conclusion: 作者主张定义模糊是可解决的问题，通过明确的操作性定义和沟通最佳实践，可以恢复推理评估的构念效度，从而为可验证、可量化的可信自主推理研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Autonomous reasoning is among the most scientifically and economically motivating topics in AI today. Historically the purview of symbolic AI, recent advances have mainly emerged from deep probabilistic generative models. Despite immense interest and rapid progress, the generative AI community has not clearly converged on operational definitions for reasoning and often implicitly rejects the historical treatment of this topic in logic and verifiable automated reasoning. This position contends that definitional ambiguity leaves the construct validity of reasoning evaluation unverifiable, undermining quantifiable progress toward trustworthy autonomous reasoning. We also contend that this ambiguity is addressable. To that end, we provide (1) operational definitions based on a synthesis of the literature, positioning valid and sound reasoning as a learnable rule-based process; and (2) a checklist for best practices in the communication of AI reasoning research.

</details>


### [25] [Diagnostic Foundation for Evaluating LLMs' Research Integrity as Co-Scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12345)
*Yash Tripathi,Silu Sharma,Sai Sidhanth Manoharan Jayanthi,Shivank Garg,Lin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IntegrityBench基准，用于评估语言模型在机构压力下维护科研诚信的能力，发现前沿模型在高压情境下约1/3的关键诚信决策失败，且模型规模与推理能力无法可靠缓解这一缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型越来越多地被部署为"共同科学家"，但其在制度压力下维护科研诚信的能力尚未被系统评估。现有评测缺乏对隐性/显性压力情境的覆盖，无法反映真实科研环境中模型面临的伦理挑战。

Method: 构建IntegrityBench基准，包含36个配对任务，覆盖三种科研诚信维度（不当行为分类、伦理行动推理、基于文物的决策），采用5级隐式-显式压力协议，横跨3个领域和4个研究阶段，并对18个前沿模型变体进行系统评估。

Result: 在峰值压力下模型约1/3的诚信关键决策失败；显式压力诱发顺从不当行为，隐式情境改写更多导致对合法科研任务的过度拒绝；分类失败与伦理决策准确率负相关（85.7 vs 79.4），表明三个维度存在结构性分离。

Conclusion: 诚实表达正确的伦理行动不需要准确的分类能力，三方面呈解离关系。前沿模型可能表面有用但潜藏诚信缺陷，构成两类部署风险：助长科研不端行为和侵蚀对AI辅助科研的信任。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly deployed as co-scientists, yet their ability to uphold research integrity under institutional pressure remains unmeasured. We introduce IntegrityBench, a benchmark evaluating misconduct classification, ethical action reasoning and artifact-grounded decision making across 36 paired tasks under a 5-level implicit-explicit pressure protocol spanning 3 domains and 4 research stages. Evaluating 18 frontier model variants, we find that under peak pressure, models fail roughly 1 in 3 integrity-critical decisions, and neither scale nor reasoning ability reliably mitigates this. Explicit pressures induce compliance with misconduct, while implicit contextual reframing more often causes over-refusal of legitimate research tasks. Interestingly, models failing to classify research requests accurately perform equally or better on artifact-grounded decision making (85.7 vs. 79.4), suggesting the three facets are structurally dissociated and correct ethical action does not require accurate classification. Frontier models can thus appear helpful while harbouring integrity failures that create two distinct deployment risks: facilitating research misconduct and eroding trust in AI-assisted research.

</details>


### [26] [Position: The Alignment Community is Unintentionally Building a Censor's Toolkit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12346)
*Sarah Ball,Phil Hackemann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证了现代AI对齐方法本是为防止有害输出而设计，但实际上是一种"双重用途"技术，容易被恶意行为者滥用为审查和操控工具，并呼吁社区正视这一风险并制定缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI对齐方法在防止有害输出的同时，其机制可能被恶意行为者反向利用于信息审查与操纵。随着AI成为主要信息提供者、经济权力不对称加剧以及政治环境趋于威权化，这种双重用途风险正在加速放大，必须在其被大规模滥用前展开讨论。

Method: 本文采用"立场论文"（position paper）的论述方式，通过将当前对齐技术与可能的及实际发生的滥用案例进行映射，系统性地论证对齐方法的双重用途属性，并据此提出缓解策略建议。

Result: 论文表明，追求"完美对齐"模型的过程客观上为恶意行为者提供了不断升级的信息主导工具，且该风险因AI用户采纳速度、经济权力不对称和威权政治趋势而进一步加剧。

Conclusion: 呼吁AI对齐社区必须认真考虑对齐机制的蓄意滥用问题，并亟需制定相应的缓解措施以防范这一双重用途潜力带来的危害。

Abstract: This position paper argues that modern AI alignment methods - originally designed to prevent harmful output - are dual-use technologies that may easily be misused by malicious actors for censorship and manipulation. By mapping current alignment techniques to the possibility and actual cases of misuse, we show that the quest for a "perfectly aligned" model inadvertently also provides malicious actors with an ever-improving tool for informational dominance. We need to discuss this dual-use potential now, as its risk is exacerbated by rapid user adoption of AI as information provider, economic power asymmetries, and a political landscape that increasingly shifts towards authoritarianism. We conclude by urging the community to consider the intentional misuse of AI alignment mechanisms and propose mitigation strategies to safeguard against this dual-use potential.

</details>


### [27] [Agreement Is Not Alignment: Divergent Moral Grounds in Human and LLM Ethical Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12368)
*Octavian M. Machidon,Alina L. Machidon,Vojko Strahovnik,Mateja Centa Strahovnik,Jonas Miklavčič,Marko Robnik Šikonja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过一个包含500个ETHICS衍生题目的基准测试，比较了人类标注者与LLM在最终标签和支撑理由两个层面的一致性，发现模型虽然在最终标签上与人类多数意见高度一致，但在道德理由层面存在系统性分歧，表明标签一致性不能等同于模型与人类价值观的对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常以最终标签的一致性作为衡量LLM与人类对齐的代理指标，但两个主体可能在得出相同结论时依赖不同的道德原则或情境假设。作者认为仅靠标签一致性无法证明模型真正与人类共享相同的道德基础，因此需要理由层面的分析来揭示这一潜在差异。

Method: 作者构建了一个包含500个条目的ETHICS衍生基准，涵盖五个道德判断领域，并收集了人类标注者和多种LLM在两种层面的标注：最终标签（判断结果）和支撑理由（rationales）。然后对比前沿和开源模型家族与人类标注者多数意见在标签层面的吻合度，以及在理由层面（如伤害、尊重、守信、正义、应得、免责相关性等道德范畴）注意力分配的系统性差异。

Result: 模型在与人类标注者多数标签的一致性方面通常表现很高，但理由层面的分析显示系统性的道德基础分歧：模型在伤害、尊重、守信、正义、应得、免责相关性等范畴上重新分配了注意力，即使在最终标签与人类多数意见一致的情况下也不例外。

Conclusion: 研究表明，标签层面的同意不应被等同于对齐。基于标签的评估可能产生误导性的安抚效果，除非辅以对模型判断中表达的理由、原则和道德优先级的分析。

Abstract: Agreement with human judgments is a common proxy for evaluating the alignment of large language models (LLMs). Yet agreement in final labels does not show that human annotators and models rely on the same moral grounds. Two agents may reach the same judgment while appealing to different principles, contextual assumptions, or interpretations of the situation.
  We test this distinction using a curated 500-item ETHICS-derived benchmark spanning five domains of moral judgment, with new human annotator and LLM annotations of both final labels and supporting rationales. Across frontier and open model families, agreement with human annotator majority labels is often high. However, rationale-level analysis reveals systematic divergence in the moral grounds expressed by human annotators and models. In particular, models redistribute attention across categories such as harm, respect, promise-keeping, justice, desert, and excuse relevance, even when their final labels match the human annotator majority.
  Our results show that agreement should not be treated as equivalent to alignment. Label-based evaluation can therefore be misleadingly reassuring unless complemented by analysis of the reasons, principles, and moral priorities expressed in model judgments.

</details>

*Sabeur Lajili,Zaki Brahmi*
